# Interactivity and accessibility (SVG 2 CR chapter 15, appendix C, and the accessibility attribute rules of chapter 5)

Part of the [W3C specification audit](../design_docs/0071-w3c_spec_audit.md). One row per element,
attribute, property, value and feature in scope. Findings come from reading Donner source at
revision `b39a524a2` and the specification versions listed below. No code was built or executed
for this ledger, so every behavioral claim is a code-reading result until a test confirms it. All
paths are relative to the repository root.


All Donner behavior statements below come from reading source; nothing was built or run.

## Specs read

| Spec | Level | Maturity | Dated URL read |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| SVG, Scripting and Interactivity | 2 | CR Snapshot 2026-10-06 | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html |
| SVG, Accessibility Support (appendix C, informative) | 2 | CR Snapshot 2026-10-06 | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/access.html |
| SVG, Document Structure (5.5.5, 5.7, 5.11.5, 5.11.6, 5.12) | 2 | CR Snapshot 2026-10-06 | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/struct.html |
| SVG, Linking (16.2 a element) | 2 | CR Snapshot 2026-10-06 | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/linking.html |
| SVG, Conformance (2.2 processing modes) | 2 | CR Snapshot 2026-10-06 | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/conform.html |
| SVG, Rendering Model (rendered and non-rendered elements, visibility) | 2 | CR Snapshot 2026-10-06 | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/render.html |
| SVG, Painting (marker interaction rule) | 2 | CR Snapshot 2026-10-06 | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/painting.html |
| SVG, Styling (user agent style sheet, anchor check only) | 2 | CR Snapshot 2026-10-06 | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/styling.html |
| CSS Basic User Interface (cursor) | 4 | Working Draft 2026-01-20 | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-ui-4-20260120/ |
| SVG Accessibility API Mappings | 1.0 | Working Draft 2026-10-21 | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-svg-aam-1.0-20261021/ |
| Pointer Events (touch-action, scope check only) | 4 | Working Draft 2026-10-08 | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-pointerevents4-20261008/ |

Notes on the baseline: `https://www.w3.org/TR/css-ui-4/`, `https://www.w3.org/TR/svg-aam-1.0/` and `https://www.w3.org/TR/pointerevents/` were fetched as the latest published versions and resolved to the dated documents above. The CR interact chapter defines one property (pointer-events) and no longer defines cursor or zoomAndPan; cursor is audited against CSS UI 4 because SVG content relies on it. WAI-ARIA, Core-AAM, Graphics-AAM, AccName, UI Events, DOM and HTML were not fetched: Donner implements none of the behaviors they define (see the accessibility, focus and event rows), so their item-level inventories are out of scope for a user agent without script, a focus model or an accessibility bridge; the SVG-side requirements that point at them each have a row.

## Inventory

Row keys in this ledger, 233 in total:

- Attributes (109): one row for every line of the CR attribute index for the accessibility and event handler attributes: `role`, 48 `aria-*` attributes and 60 `on*` event handler attributes. Keys are `attr:<name>@<element list copied from the index>`.
- Properties: `prop:pointer-events` with one `val:` row for each of its 11 keywords (the only property the CR interact chapter and property index define for this area); `prop:cursor` from CSS UI 4 with one `val:` row for each of the 36 `<cursor-predefined>` keywords plus `<url>`, `<url-set>` and the `<number>{2}` hotspot from `<cursor-image>`; `prop:touch-action` (not in the CR, recorded as out of scope).
- Features: derived by walking interact.html section by section (15.2 supported events, 15.2.1, 15.3, 15.4, 15.5.1, 15.5.2 steps 1 to 7, 15.6 including the text, image, foreignObject, clip, mask, filter and opacity rules, 15.7 paragraph by paragraph, 15.8, 15.9), struct.html 5.5.5, 5.7, 5.11.5, 5.11.6 and 5.12, linking.html 16.2 (interaction sentences only), conform.html 2.2.1 (script execution and interaction features), painting.html marker interaction, and SVG-AAM sections 4.1.1, 4.1.2, 5 and 7.1.
- Out of scope and why: DOM interfaces (SVGScriptElement, GlobalEventHandlers IDL, addEventListener) because Donner exposes a C++ API and runs no script; the `script` element attribute rows (`type`, `href`, `crossorigin`) and the `a` element attribute rows belong to `structure.md`; `tabindex` and `autofocus` index lines also belong to `structure.md`, their behavior is covered here by the focus feature rows.

## Matrix

### Attributes: role and aria-* (accessibility)

| Key | Class | Spec anchor | Requirement (paraphrase) | Parse evidence | Consume evidence | Tests | Impact | Size | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| attr:role@a, circle, ellipse, foreignObject, g, image, line, path, polygon, polyline, rect, svg, switch, symbol, text, textPath, tspan, use, view | partial | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/struct.html#RoleAttribute | Space-separated role tokens; the element takes the first valid, non-abstract role allowed for its type. The lacuna value is the element default implied semantics. Ignored on non-rendered elements. Not animatable. | Generic attribute store, verified once and shared by every row in this group: no element parser claims the name, so it falls through `donner/svg/parser/AttributeParser.cc:404-423` (`ParseCommonAttribute`) to `:387-400` and `:335-362`, then `donner/svg/SVGElement.cc:753-812` stores it in `AttributesComponent` (`:808-811`). Elements with no Donner type (`script`, `foreignObject`, `view`) take the same path through `ParseAttributeGeneric` (`AttributeParser.cc:2287-2291`, `:2337-2345`). | None beyond generic lookup. Round trip: the original source text is kept verbatim (`donner/svg/SVGDocument.h:330`) and `XMLNode::serializeToString` writes every stored attribute (`donner/base/xml/XMLNode.cc:1041-1056`). The editor parses with user attributes enabled (`donner/editor/EditorParseOptions.h:18`). Dropped with an "Unknown attribute" warning only when the embedder sets `Options::disableUserAttributes` (`AttributeParser.cc:351-360`, default false at `donner/svg/parser/SVGParser.h:78`). | Mechanism test: `SVGParser.Attributes` (`donner/svg/parser/tests/SVGParser_tests.cc:410-446`) asserts a non-presentation attribute is readable when user attributes are enabled and removed with a warning when disabled. No test names this attribute. | low | L | Preserved, not rejected, never interpreted. The only consumers are generic: `SVGElement::getAttribute` and CSS attribute selectors (for example `[aria-hidden="true"]`), which match any stored attribute. No accessibility tree, name computation or platform mapping exists (search for `aria`, `role`, `accessib`, `a11y` under `donner/svg`, `donner/css`, `donner/editor` finds only documentation comments and an editor export marker `data-donner-export-role`). Value types are not validated. Group verified by reading the shared store path and searching for each family name; no per-attribute code exists. Token splitting, role validation, the per-element default and the non-rendered exclusion have no implementation. Size is the shared accessibility-export work item (see feat:svg-aam-1.0/role-mapping). |

### Property: pointer-events

| Key | Class | Spec anchor | Requirement (paraphrase) | Parse evidence | Consume evidence | Tests | Impact | Size | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| prop:pointer-events | partial | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#PointerEventsProperty | Value is one of eleven keywords; initial auto; applies to container elements, graphics elements and use; inherited; computed as specified; discrete animation. Usable as a presentation attribute and in CSS. | `donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.cc:1822-1858` (`ParsePointerEvents`, single identifier, ASCII case-insensitive); registered as presentation attribute and CSS property at `donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.cc:2479` and `:3185-3192`; inherited with initial auto at `donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.h:328-329`. An invalid value is a parse error, so the declaration is ignored. | `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1520-1537` (`configFromPointerEvents`), `:1760-1794` (`prepareHitGeometry`), `:1711-1727` (`HitGeometry::contains`). Read only by hit testing. `HitTestMode::EditorSelection` (`donner/svg/core/HitTestMode.h:9-12`, used by `donner/editor/EditorApp.cc:2347-2350`) deliberately ignores the property so editor picking works on `pointer-events:none` art. | `PropertyRegistry` parse cases at `donner/svg/properties/tests/PropertyRegistry_tests.cc:1624-1639`, `:1754-1768`, `:1807`; consume tests `donner/svg/renderer/tests/RenderingContext_tests.cc:979-1058`, `donner/svg/tests/DonnerController_tests.cc:282-292`. | medium | M | All eleven keywords parse and select the right fill, stroke, visibility and paint tests for shapes. Partial because the hit regions those keywords select are wrong for images, text, clipped content and strokes (see the val rows and the hit-testing feature rows). Discrete animation of the property was not verified (unverified). The value set and visibility rules are conformant for parsing and for filled shapes only. |
| val:pointer-events/auto | conformant | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#PointerEventsProperty | Same as visiblePainted. | `donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.cc:1822-1858` (`ParsePointerEvents`, single identifier, ASCII case-insensitive) | `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1522-1523` maps Auto and VisiblePainted to the same configuration. | `PointerEventsAutoUsesVisiblePaintedSemantics` (`donner/svg/renderer/tests/RenderingContext_tests.cc:992-1006`); parse `donner/svg/properties/tests/PropertyRegistry_tests.cc:1625`, `:1807`. | - | - | Shape behavior only; image and text defects are in the feature rows. |
| val:pointer-events/bounding-box | partial | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#PointerEventsProperty | The element is a target when the pointer is over its bounding box. | `donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.cc:1822-1858` (`ParsePointerEvents`, single identifier, ASCII case-insensitive) | `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1783-1788` and `:1716-1720`: the test rectangle is the axis-aligned box of the transformed geometry in document space, inflated by the stroke width when stroke is painted. | `PointerEventsBoundingBoxHitsWithoutPaint` (`donner/svg/renderer/tests/RenderingContext_tests.cc:979-990`), unrotated rect only; parse `donner/svg/properties/tests/PropertyRegistry_tests.cc:1627`. | low | S | For a rotated or skewed element the hit area is the enclosing document-space rectangle, larger than the object bounding box, and it grows with stroke width although the object bounding box excludes stroke. Because `getShapeWorldBounds` unions descendant geometry (`donner/svg/components/shape/ShapeSystem.cc:1241-1253`), a `g` or the root `svg` carrying this value also becomes a target over the union of its children. Text uses the ink box. This is the only value under which `image` can be hit. |
| val:pointer-events/visiblePainted | conformant | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#PointerEventsProperty | Target when visibility is visible and the pointer is over the fill with fill not none, or over the stroke with stroke not none. | `donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.cc:1822-1858` (`ParsePointerEvents`, single identifier, ASCII case-insensitive) | `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1523`, paint requirement applied at `:1732-1736`, visibility at `:1773`. | Same code path as auto: `PointerEventsAutoUsesVisiblePaintedSemantics` (`donner/svg/renderer/tests/RenderingContext_tests.cc:992-1006`); parse `donner/svg/properties/tests/PropertyRegistry_tests.cc:1628`. | - | - | "Painted" is decided from the computed paint not being `none` (`donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1732-1733`); a `url()` reference that fails to resolve with no fallback still counts as painted, where the spec speaks of the actual value. Shares the stroke-region defects recorded under feat:svg2/interact-hit-stroke-region. |
| val:pointer-events/visibleFill | conformant | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#PointerEventsProperty | Target when visibility is visible and the pointer is over the interior; the fill value is irrelevant. | `donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.cc:1822-1858` (`ParsePointerEvents`, single identifier, ASCII case-insensitive) | `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1528`. | `PointerEventsVisibleFillHitsTransparentFill` (`donner/svg/renderer/tests/RenderingContext_tests.cc:1008-1019`); parse `donner/svg/properties/tests/PropertyRegistry_tests.cc:1754-1755`. | - | - | The test uses fill-opacity 0, not fill none; fill none takes the same branch by code reading. |
| val:pointer-events/visibleStroke | divergent | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#PointerEventsProperty | Target when visibility is visible and the pointer is over the perimeter; the stroke value is irrelevant. | `donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.cc:1822-1858` (`ParsePointerEvents`, single identifier, ASCII case-insensitive) | `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1529`, but `:1734` sets the stroke width to 0 when stroke is none. | `PointerEventsVisibleStrokeRequiresVisibility` (`donner/svg/renderer/tests/RenderingContext_tests.cc:1033-1044`) covers only the hidden case; parse `donner/svg/properties/tests/PropertyRegistry_tests.cc:1760-1761`. | low | S | With `stroke="none"` the perimeter can never be hit (distance must be 0). Shares the stroke-region defects recorded under feat:svg2/interact-hit-stroke-region. |
| val:pointer-events/visible | partial | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#PointerEventsProperty | Target when visibility is visible and the pointer is over the interior or the perimeter; fill and stroke values are irrelevant. | `donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.cc:1822-1858` (`ParsePointerEvents`, single identifier, ASCII case-insensitive) | `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1530`; interior half works, perimeter half fails when stroke is none (`:1734`). | None for hit testing; parse `donner/svg/properties/tests/PropertyRegistry_tests.cc:1629`. | low | S | Same root cause as visibleStroke. |
| val:pointer-events/painted | conformant | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#PointerEventsProperty | Target over painted fill or painted stroke; visibility is irrelevant. | `donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.cc:1822-1858` (`ParsePointerEvents`, single identifier, ASCII case-insensitive) | `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1531`. | `PointerEventsPaintedRequiresVisiblePaint` (`donner/svg/renderer/tests/RenderingContext_tests.cc:1021-1031`) asserts the unpainted miss; parse `donner/svg/properties/tests/PropertyRegistry_tests.cc:1630`. | - | - | The hidden-but-painted hit is not asserted for this keyword (it is for stroke). Shares the stroke-region defects recorded under feat:svg2/interact-hit-stroke-region. |
| val:pointer-events/fill | conformant-untested | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#PointerEventsProperty | Target over the interior; fill and visibility are irrelevant. | `donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.cc:1822-1858` (`ParsePointerEvents`, single identifier, ASCII case-insensitive) | `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1532`. | None for hit testing; parse `donner/svg/properties/tests/PropertyRegistry_tests.cc:1631`. | - | - |  |
| val:pointer-events/stroke | partial | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#PointerEventsProperty | Target over the perimeter; stroke and visibility are irrelevant. | `donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.cc:1822-1858` (`ParsePointerEvents`, single identifier, ASCII case-insensitive) | `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1533`; fails when stroke is none (`:1734`). | `PointerEventsStrokeHitsHiddenStrokeGeometry` (`donner/svg/renderer/tests/RenderingContext_tests.cc:1046-1059`), painted stroke only; parse `donner/svg/properties/tests/PropertyRegistry_tests.cc:1632`. | low | S | Hidden painted strokes hit as required; unpainted strokes never hit. ### Property: cursor (CSS Basic User Interface 4; not in the SVG 2 CR property index)

| Key | Class | Spec anchor | Requirement (paraphrase) | Parse evidence | Consume evidence | Tests | Impact | Size | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| prop:cursor | partial | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/WD-css-ui-4-20260120/#cursor | Value is zero or more cursor images, each followed by a comma, then a mandatory predefined keyword. Initial auto; applies to all elements; inherited; computed as specified with relative URLs made absolute; discrete animation. | `donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.cc:1865-1920` (keyword table and `ParseCursorType`), `:1922-1979` (`ParseCursor`); presentation attribute and CSS registration at `donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.cc:2448`, `:3194-3201`; inherited, initial empty image list with auto at `donner/svg/properties/PropertyRegistry.h:333-334`. A value without the keyword is a parse error and the declaration is ignored. | `donner/svg/DonnerController.cc:107-119` (`cursorAt` returns the computed `Cursor` of the frontmost hit entity; the host applies it). No caller inside the editor or the examples (search for `cursorAt` under `donner/editor`, `examples`, `tools` finds none), so only embedders consume it. | `CursorKeywordIsTyped` (`donner/svg/properties/tests/PropertyRegistry_tests.cc:1814-1874`), `CursorUrlFallbackListIsTyped` (`:1876-1898`), `CursorAtReturnsInheritedTypedValue` (`donner/svg/tests/DonnerController_tests.cc:294-318`), `CursorOverUseUsesTheInstanceStyle` (`:489-500`). | low | S | The SVG 2 CR no longer defines cursor; it comes from CSS UI. Gaps: no `<url-set>` form, URLs are returned as authored rather than absolutized, cursor images are never fetched or decoded (the spec requires PNG and static SVG support of the user agent, here left to the host), and the SVG user agent rule `:link, :visited { cursor: pointer }` never applies (see feat:css-ui-4/cursor-ua-link-rule). The grammar is otherwise conformant. |

The rule that auto acts as `text` over selectable text and `default` elsewhere is left to the host; Donner does not resolve it. |

### Features: hit testing (SVG 2 15.4 to 15.6)

| Key | Class | Spec anchor | Requirement (paraphrase) | Parse evidence | Consume evidence | Tests | Impact | Size | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| feat:svg2/interact-hit-topmost-target | conformant | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#PointerEvents | The target is the topmost graphics element whose relevant graphical content is under the pointer. | n/a (query API): `donner/svg/DonnerController.cc:67-86` (`findIntersecting`), `:88-105` (`findAllIntersecting`), `:46-63` (`captureHitTest`). | `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1831-1844`: reverse scan over render instances, which are created in paint order (`:471-472`). | `OverlappingTopLinkWins` (`donner/svg/tests/DonnerController_tests.cc:110`), `TopUnlinkedShapeOccludesLink` (`:129`), `FindAllIntersecting` (`donner/svg/renderer/tests/RenderingContext_tests.cc:308`), `TransformedCoordinates` (`donner/svg/tests/DonnerController_tests.cc:152`). | - | - | Brute-force scan; ordering relies on instance creation order equal to paint order (by code reading). |
| feat:svg2/interact-hit-no-target-ignored | conformant | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#PointerEvents | When no element is hit the event is ignored (or falls to window behavior). | n/a | `donner/svg/DonnerController.cc:73-75`, `:85` return no element. | `ClickOnEmptyArea` (`donner/svg/tests/DonnerController_tests.cc:92`), `FindIntersectingMissesEmptyArea` (`donner/svg/renderer/tests/RenderingContext_tests.cc:294`). | - | - |  |
| feat:svg2/interact-hit-fill-region | conformant | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#PointerEventsProperty | The interior region is the area the fill would cover under the fill rule. | n/a | `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1725` with `fill-rule` from `:1737`; `donner/base/Path.cc:1281-1296` (winding number, nonzero or evenodd). | `FindIntersectingHitsRect` (`donner/svg/renderer/tests/RenderingContext_tests.cc:280`), `PointerEventsVisibleFillHitsTransparentFill` (`:1008`); `isInside` unit tests in `donner/base/tests/Path_tests.cc`. | - | - | Points within 0.1 user units outside the outline also count as inside (`donner/base/Path.h:225-232`), a small fixed inflation that is not scaled by zoom. No hit test exercises `fill-rule: evenodd` through the public hit-test API. |
| feat:svg2/interact-hit-stroke-region | divergent | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#PointerEventsProperty | The perimeter region is the area the stroke would cover. | n/a | `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1726` calls `Path::isOnPath(point, strokeWidth)`; `donner/base/Path.h:235-243` and `donner/base/Path.cc:1218-1277` compare the distance to the centerline against the full stroke width. | `PointerEventsStrokeHitsHiddenStrokeGeometry` (`donner/svg/renderer/tests/RenderingContext_tests.cc:1046`) passes because its probe lies inside the true stroke. | low | M | The hit band is twice the painted half-width on each side. Line caps, joins, miter limit, dashes, `vector-effect: non-scaling-stroke` and the transform scale of a non-uniform matrix are not modeled (round-capped distance to segments). The width used is the raw number of the computed length (`donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1734`, `.value().value`), so percentage or font-relative stroke widths are not resolved. |
| feat:svg2/interact-hit-stroke-ignores-stroke-value | divergent | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#PointerEventsProperty | For visibleStroke, visible, stroke and all, the stroke value does not affect event processing. | n/a | `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1734`: stroke width becomes 0 when stroke is none, so `isOnPath` needs distance 0. | None (existing stroke tests use a painted stroke). | low | S |  |
| feat:svg2/interact-hit-markers | conformant | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/painting.html#MarkerElement | Markers cannot be interacted with: no event is dispatched to a marker or its children when the pointer is over rendered marker content. | n/a | `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1750-1758` and `:1764`: marker content lives in a detached offscreen shadow tree whose root has no parent, so `EventTargetEntity` yields no target and the instance is skipped. | `PatternAndMarkerContentIsNotAHitTarget` (`donner/svg/tests/DonnerController_tests.cc:504-527`). | - | - | The referencing path is hit only by its own geometry; marker ink does not extend its hit area. |
| feat:svg2/interact-hit-text-character-cells | divergent | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#PointerEventsProperty | Text is hit-tested per character cell: any point inside a character cell of the text string counts. | n/a | `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1740-1747`, `:1779-1782`, `:1712-1714`: one ink bounding box for the whole `text` root, taken from `TextEngine::computedInkBounds` (`donner/svg/text/TextEngine.cc:8641`). | `TextPathHitTestUsesTransformedGlyphBounds` (`donner/svg/renderer/tests/RenderingContext_tests.cc:323-361`) pins the ink-box behavior. | medium | M | The union ink box covers gaps between lines, between glyphs and inside text on a path, and misses cell area with no ink (spaces, side bearings, ascent above short glyphs). Only the `text` root is ever returned: `tspan`, `textPath` and an `a` inside text are never the target because only the root has text bounds and the others have no path (`:1783-1793`, `:1722-1724`). A per-character query already exists (`TextEngine::getCharNumAtPosition`, `donner/svg/text/TextEngine.h:248`) but hit testing does not use it. |
| feat:svg2/interact-hit-text-paint-and-visibility | divergent | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#PointerEventsProperty | For text, visiblePainted and painted require fill or stroke other than none; the visible* values require visibility visible; fill, stroke and all always hit. | n/a | `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1712-1714`: the text branch returns before `testFill` and `testStroke` are consulted; visibility is honored at `:1773`. | None. | low | S | Text with `fill="none" stroke="none"` still hits under auto, visiblePainted and painted. Visibility uses the instance flag, which stays true for a hidden root that paints a visible span (`:327-347`, `:509-511`). |
| feat:svg2/interact-hit-image-rectangle | missing | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#PointerEventsProperty | Raster images are hit-tested on the whole image rectangle, with visibility gating for the visible* values. | n/a | `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1783-1793`: an `image` has bounds (`donner/svg/components/shape/ShapeSystem.cc:830-847`) but no path (`:1002-1030`), so `contains` returns false at `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1722-1724` unless the value is bounding-box. | None (search for `<image` in `donner/svg/renderer/tests/RenderingContext_tests.cc` and `donner/svg/tests/DonnerController_tests.cc` finds no hit test). | medium | S | An `image` is hittable only with `pointer-events: bounding-box`; a link wrapping a bitmap never resolves, and the editor cannot pick images through this path. |
| feat:svg2/interact-hit-image-transparent-pixels | missing | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#PointerEventsProperty | Image hits do not depend on pixel content: transparent pixels inside the image rectangle still hit. | n/a | Same code as the row above; no pixel lookup exists, and no rectangle test exists either. | None. | low | S | Fixed by the same change as the image rectangle row; the correct implementation needs no alpha test. |
| feat:svg2/interact-hit-foreignObject | missing | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#PointerEventsProperty | foreignObject is hit-tested on its rectangular object bounding box, like an image. | `foreignObject` has no element type (`donner/svg/ElementType.h:15-75`) and is created as an unknown element (`donner/svg/parser/SVGParser.cc:794-796`). | Unknown elements get no render instance (`donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:227-230`, `:407-409`), so they are never hit. | None for hit testing; `donner/svg/parser/tests/SVGParser_tests.cc:720-741` shows the element is Unknown. | low | M | Follows from foreignObject being unimplemented (see `structure.md`). |
| feat:svg2/interact-hit-clip-path | divergent | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#PointerEventsProp | Pointer events are captured over the rendered area of a clipped element and must not be captured over the clipped-away area. | n/a | `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1760-1794`: hit geometry is built without reading the instance clip path (`:517-520` attaches it for painting only). | None (`GradientAndClipPath`, `donner/svg/renderer/tests/RenderingContext_tests.cc:656`, does not hit test). | medium | M | A shape clipped to a small region still captures hits over its full geometry, hiding elements below. |
| feat:svg2/interact-hit-overflow-clip | spec-ambiguity | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#PointerEventsProp | The chapter states the clipping rule for clip paths; it does not say whether viewport overflow clipping of nested svg, symbol or image content also removes hit area. | n/a | `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:462-481` records `instance.clipRect` for painting; `:1760-1794` never reads it. | None. | low | S | Donner hits content outside a clipping viewport. Browsers treat overflow clipping as removing hit area; the CR text does not state it. |
| feat:svg2/interact-hit-mask | conformant-untested | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#PointerEventsProp | A mask does not restrict pointer events: masked-out areas still capture them. | n/a | `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1760-1794` does not read `instance.mask`. | None (`MaskElement`, `donner/svg/renderer/tests/RenderingContext_tests.cc:963`, only builds the tree). | - | - | Conformant by omission. |
| feat:svg2/interact-hit-filter | conformant-untested | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#PointerEventsProp | The filter property has no effect on pointer events. | n/a | `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1760-1794` does not read `instance.resolvedFilter`. | None. | - | - | Conformant by omission; filter-expanded or filter-hidden pixels do not change the hit region. |
| feat:svg2/interact-hit-opacity | conformant | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#PointerEventsProperty | opacity, fill-opacity and stroke-opacity do not affect event processing. | n/a | `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1730-1738` reads only whether paint is none, never an opacity. | `PointerEventsVisibleFillHitsTransparentFill` (`donner/svg/renderer/tests/RenderingContext_tests.cc:1008`) covers fill-opacity 0 only. | - | - | `opacity: 0` and `stroke-opacity: 0` take the same path by code reading but have no test. |
| feat:svg2/interact-hit-visibility | conformant | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/render.html#VisibilityControl | A visibility:hidden element stays in the rendering tree and may still be sensitive to pointer events depending on the pointer-events value. | n/a | `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1773` gates only the values whose configuration sets `requireVisible` (`:1520-1534`); the flag comes from `:509-511`. | `PointerEventsVisibleStrokeRequiresVisibility` (`donner/svg/renderer/tests/RenderingContext_tests.cc:1033`), `PointerEventsStrokeHitsHiddenStrokeGeometry` (`:1046`). | - | - |  |
| feat:svg2/interact-hit-display-none | conformant | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#PointerEvents | An element with display none, or with an ancestor with display none, cannot be a target. | n/a | `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:441-443`: no render instance is created for display none and its subtree is not traversed; `:1763-1766` requires an instance. | `DisplayNoneNotIntersectable` (`donner/svg/renderer/tests/RenderingContext_tests.cc:935`), `DisplayNoneSuppressesLink` (`donner/svg/tests/DonnerController_tests.cc:268`). | - | - |  |
| feat:svg2/interact-hit-never-rendered | conformant | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/render.html#Rendered-vs-NonRendered | Non-rendered elements (defs content, pattern, clipPath, mask, marker, symbol definitions, descriptive elements) are not sensitive to pointer events. | n/a | `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:426-438` skips Nonrenderable elements and the light-tree children of shadow-only containers; offscreen shadow roots resolve to no target (`:1750-1758`). | `PatternAndMarkerContentIsNotAHitTarget` (`donner/svg/tests/DonnerController_tests.cc:504`). | - | - | No test probes a point over `defs` or `clipPath` child geometry directly. |
| feat:svg2/interact-hit-root-svg-not-target | partial | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#hit-testing | The svg element is not a graphics element; in a stand-alone file the outermost svg is never the target, though events bubble to it. | n/a | `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1783-1793`: an `svg` has no path, so it misses by default. `donner/svg/SVGGraphicsElement.h:33-42` lists SVG, G, Defs, Switch and Symbol as valid targets, and `:1788` makes any of them hit under bounding-box through the union of descendant bounds. | `ClickOnEmptyArea` (`donner/svg/tests/DonnerController_tests.cc:92`), `FindIntersectingMissesEmptyArea` (`donner/svg/renderer/tests/RenderingContext_tests.cc:294`) cover the default. | low | S | With `pointer-events="bounding-box"` on the root (or inherited from it), a point between child shapes returns the root `svg` or a `g`. Bubbling exists only as the ancestor path used for animation triggers (`donner/svg/components/animation/AnimationSystem.cc:765-776`). |
| feat:svg2/interact-hit-nested-svg-not-target | conformant-untested | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#hit-testing | A nested svg element is a container, not a graphics element, so its background area is not a hit target. | n/a | Same mechanism as the root row: no path, so no hit unless bounding-box (`donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1783-1793`). | None. | - | - | Shares the bounding-box edge case of the root row. |
| feat:svg2/interact-hit-use-shadow-target | conformant | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/struct.html#UseEventHandling | Elements in a use shadow tree can be event targets; when the event leaves the shadow tree it is retargeted so outside observers see the use element. | n/a | `donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1750-1758` walks a shadow entity up to the element that instantiated it; `donner/svg/DonnerController.cc:10-21` returns that element; duplicates are collapsed at `:96-103`. | `CapturedHitOverUseTargetsTheUseElement` (`donner/svg/tests/DonnerController_tests.cc:393`), `FindIntersectingOverUseTargetsTheUseElement` (`:404`), `FindAllIntersectingReportsAUseElementOnce` (`:416`), `NestedUseTargetsTheOutermostUseElement` (`:433`), `UseTargetIsStableAcrossAnimationTicks` (`:451`). | - | - | The public API always reports the retargeted (light tree) view. The instance element inside the shadow tree and its corresponding element are not exposed. |
| feat:svg2/interact-use-instance-listeners | partial | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/struct.html#UseEventHandling | Listeners on a referenced element also listen on each of its instances in use shadow trees, including event attributes. | n/a | Hits are reported on the `use` host only (`donner/svg/DonnerController.cc:10-21`), and animation triggers match the dispatched target and its ancestors (`donner/svg/components/animation/AnimationSystem.cc:765-776`, `:1002`). | None. | low | M | By code reading, an animation with `begin="shape.click"` on a referenced element does not start when a use instance of it is clicked, because the dispatch path starts at the `use` element. Not exercised by a test (unverified beyond code reading). Script listeners are out of scope. |

### Features: links (SVG 2 16.2, interaction side)

| Key | Class | Spec anchor | Requirement (paraphrase) | Parse evidence | Consume evidence | Tests | Impact | Size | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| feat:svg2/interact-a-link-hit-region | conformant | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/linking.html#Links | For pointer events a linked hit region exists for each rendered element inside the a element, according to each element pointer-events value, not for the bounding box of the a element. | `donner/svg/SVGAElement.h:100-111` (`href`, with `xlink:href` alias). | `donner/svg/DonnerController.cc:121-143` (`hitTestLink`): ordinary hit test, then walk ancestors to the nearest `a` with an href. | `ClickInsideLinkDirectChild` (`donner/svg/tests/DonnerController_tests.cc:38`), `ClickOnNestedDescendant` (`:59`), `ClickOutsideAnyLink` (`:80`), `PointerEventsNoneSuppressesLink` (`:282`), `LinkEnclosingUseResolvesThroughTheUseElement` (`:474`), `LinkWithoutHrefIsNotALink` (`:208`). | - | - | Inherits every hit-region defect above (images, text, clips). |
| feat:svg2/interact-a-nested-links-inert | divergent | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/linking.html#Links | Links may not nest: an a element that descends from another link must have its href ignored and be treated as inactive, while still rendering as a container. | n/a | `donner/svg/DonnerController.cc:131-140` returns the innermost `a` that has an href. | `NestedLinksResolveToInnermost` (`donner/svg/tests/DonnerController_tests.cc:232-246`) asserts the divergent result; `TargetlessInnerLinkFallsThroughToOuter` (`:250`). | low | S | The spec result is the outer link. The CR marks rendering of invalid nested links as at risk, not the inert rule. |
| feat:svg2/interact-a-activation | partial | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/linking.html#Links | On activation (click, keyboard or other input) the user agent should fetch the resource and display or download it; all links must be focusable and keyboard-activatable. | n/a | `donner/svg/DonnerController.h:59-172`: the stateless `hitTestLink` returns the `a` element, the authored href and the hit element; Donner never navigates. | `HrefFragmentReturnedRaw` (`donner/svg/tests/DonnerController_tests.cc:173`), `HrefRelativeReturnedRaw` (`:184`), `HrefAbsoluteViaXlinkReturnedRaw` (`:195`), `EmptyHrefIsNotALink` (`:220`), hover tests `:322-371`. | low | M | Navigation is a host decision by design. Missing pieces a host cannot derive from this API: keyboard activation and focus (see focus rows), `target`, `download`, `rel` and related attributes as typed values (see `structure.md`), and suppression of links in non-interactive processing modes (see feat:svg2/conform-interaction-disabled). |
| feat:svg2/interact-a-link-in-text | divergent | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/linking.html#Links | The same a element serves graphical and textual links; a link inside text is activated over its own text. | n/a | Text hits resolve to the `text` root only (`donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:1740-1747`, `:1779-1782`), so the ancestor walk in `donner/svg/DonnerController.cc:131-140` starts above an inline `a`. | None. | low | M | An `a` that wraps a whole `text` element works; an `a` inside `text` or `tspan` is never reported. Depends on per-character hit testing. |

### Features: focus (SVG 2 15.7 and struct 5.11.5, 5.11.6)

| Key | Class | Spec anchor | Requirement (paraphrase) | Parse evidence | Consume evidence | Tests | Impact | Size | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| feat:svg2/interact-focus-model | missing | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#Focus | At most one element per document is focused; it receives keyboard events and matches :focus. Interactive user agents must show a focus indication for keyboard-driven focus changes. | Absent: search for `tabindex`, `autofocus`, `focus`, `focusable`, `focused` under `donner/svg` and `donner/css` (excluding tests) finds only radial-gradient focal attributes and comments. `tabindex` and `autofocus` are kept as generic attributes by the store path described in the attribute rows. | None. | None. | low | M | No focus state, no keyboard event type (`donner/svg/core/Event.h:18-29` lists mouse and wheel only), no focus ring. The editor has its own widget focus, unrelated to document focus. |
| feat:svg2/interact-focus-focusable-set | missing | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#TermFocusable | Focusable: the document root, scrollable regions, any directly rendered element with a valid integer tabindex, and every a element that is a valid link; instances in use shadow trees too. | Absent: search for `tabindex`, `autofocus`, `focus`, `focusable`, `focused` under `donner/svg` and `donner/css` (excluding tests) finds only radial-gradient focal attributes and comments. `tabindex` and `autofocus` are kept as generic attributes by the store path described in the attribute rows. | None. | None. | low | M | A host can build the list itself from `querySelector("[tabindex]")` and `a[href]`, but nothing validates the integer or checks that the element is rendered. |

### Features: events and event processing (SVG 2 15.2, 15.3, 15.5.2, 15.8)

| Key | Class | Spec anchor | Requirement (paraphrase) | Parse evidence | Consume evidence | Tests | Impact | Size | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| feat:svg2/interact-events-supported-types | partial | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#RelationshipWithUIEVENTS | Conforming software supports every non-deprecated UI Events and Clipboard event type that could occur in its use; non-interactive software should still support events that fire without interaction, such as load and error. | `donner/svg/core/Event.h:18-29` (`EventType`: click, dblclick, mousedown, mouseup, mousemove, mouseenter, mouseleave, mouseover, mouseout, wheel). `Event`, `ListenerHandle` and `EventCallback` are declared (`:59-104`) but nothing registers or invokes listeners (search for `EventCallback`, `ListenerHandle` outside that header finds nothing). | `donner/svg/SVGDocument.h:486-503` (`dispatchMouseEvent`, `dispatchAnimationEvent`), implemented at `donner/svg/SVGDocument.cc:834-856` and `donner/svg/components/animation/AnimationSystem.cc:984-1010`. Events are delivered only to declarative animation timing; arbitrary names up to 128 bytes are accepted. The editor preview synthesizes mouseout, mouseleave, mouseover, mouseenter, mousedown, mouseup, click and dblclick from pointer input (`donner/editor/EditorShell.cc:7211-7262`). | `donner/svg/tests/SVGDocument_tests.cc:2762-2800`, `:2999-3009`; `donner/svg/parser/tests/AnimateElement_tests.cc:1406`. | low | L | C++ equivalent of the event list: ten pointer event names for animation triggers plus a free-form named event. Missing: keyboard, focus, input, composition, clipboard, drag and pointer-event types, an event object delivered to host listeners, capture and cancel semantics. `mousemove` and `wheel` exist in the enum; whether the editor preview forwards them was not traced (unverified). |

| feat:svg2/interact-events-error | missing | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#SVGEvents | error is dispatched when a structurally external element fails to load or a script fails; it bubbles and is not cancelable. | n/a | Resource failures surface as parse warnings through the warning sink, not as events. | None. | low | S |  |
| feat:svg2/interact-events-unload | not-applicable | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#SVGEvents | unload applies to the outermost svg when the document is removed from a window or frame. | n/a | No window or frame concept in the engine. | None. | - | - | Host scope. |
| feat:svg2/interact-events-animation-events | partial | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#SVGEvents | beginEvent, endEvent and repeatEvent fire on animation elements when they begin, end or repeat; their on* attributes apply only to animation elements. | n/a | `donner/svg/SVGDocument.h:520-527` (`animationEvents(from, to)` returns lifecycle boundaries as values instead of dispatching). | Not re-verified here; `animation.md` owns the tests. | low | M | A value batch replaces DOM dispatch, so `onbegin`, `onend`, `onrepeat` stay inert. Using these as event-base triggers of other animations is covered in `animation.md`. |

No capture phase exists, which matches the timing rule. | No test found that asserts the non-bubbling of mouseenter or mouseleave by name. | - | - |  |
| feat:svg2/interact-event-processing-order | partial | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#event-processing | After dispatch, processing for the target proceeds in order: stop if default prevented, show title or description, apply dynamic pseudo-classes, resolve event-based animation timing, traverse a hyperlink, run text selection, then user-agent UI such as a context menu. | n/a | Only two steps have engine support, as independent host-driven calls: animation timing (`donner/svg/SVGDocument.cc:834-856`) and link lookup (`donner/svg/DonnerController.cc:121-143`). Ordering between them is whatever the host does; the editor preview dispatches animation events itself (`donner/editor/EditorShell.cc:7211-7262`). | None for ordering. | low | M | No prevent-default (no listeners), no tooltip step, no pseudo-class step, no text selection step. |

The editor does not show SVG titles as tooltips (search for `ElementType::Title` under `donner/editor` finds only an exclusion at `donner/editor/CapturedPresentation.cc:62-63`). | `SVGTitleElementTests.TextContentAccessible`, `NestedTitleInShape` (`donner/svg/tests/SVGTitleElement_tests.cc:24`, `:47`). | low | S | Should-level. |

### Features: script element and processing modes (SVG 2 15.9, conformance 2.2)

| Key | Class | Spec anchor | Requirement (paraphrase) | Parse evidence | Consume evidence | Tests | Impact | Size | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| feat:svg2/interact-script-never-rendered | conformant-untested | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#ScriptElement | script content is never rendered; the user agent style sheet forces display none with importance over any author rule. | `script` has no element type (`donner/svg/ElementType.h:15-75`); it is created as an unknown element in the SVG namespace (`donner/svg/parser/SVGParser.cc:781-797`). | Unknown elements never get a render instance (`donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:227-230`, `:407-409`), independent of CSS, so no author rule can make them render. | No pixel test for a top-level `script`. `SVGParser.BrowserActiveMarkupIsInertInProjectionButRetainedInSource` (`donner/svg/parser/tests/SVGParser_tests.cc:720-741`) asserts `script` is Unknown. | - | - | Achieved structurally rather than by a UA style rule; `getComputedStyle`-style queries would not report display none. |
| feat:svg2/interact-script-not-executed | conformant | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#ScriptElementExecution | Before executing a script the type is inspected; if the user agent does not support the scripting language, the script must not be executed. | Element retained with its text and attributes; nothing interprets `type`, `href` or `crossorigin`. | No script engine: `donner/svg/core/ProcessingMode.h:8-17`, `donner/svg/SVGDocument.h:486-503` ("Does not execute scripts"). | `SVGParser.BrowserActiveMarkupIsInertInProjectionButRetainedInSource` (`donner/svg/parser/tests/SVGParser_tests.cc:720-749`). | - | - | No scripting language is supported, so the rule is met for every `type`. External script `href` is never fetched. |
| feat:svg2/conform-script-execution-disabled | conformant | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/conform.html#features | With script execution disabled, no script in the document may run: script elements, event attributes, or script from other platform features. | n/a | Same as above; also `javascript:` URLs in links are returned as data, never evaluated (`donner/svg/DonnerController.cc:131-136`). | `donner/svg/parser/tests/SVGParser_tests.cc:720-749`. | - | - | Holds in all three Donner modes. |
| feat:svg2/conform-interaction-disabled | partial | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/conform.html#features | With interaction disabled (animated, static and both secure modes), user input events aimed at the document must have no effect: no DOM events, text selection, focus change, link traversal or interaction-triggered animation. | `donner/svg/core/ProcessingMode.h:18-28`; selected through `SVGDocument::Settings::processingMode` (`donner/svg/SVGDocument.h:845-846`). | `donner/svg/SVGDocument.cc:846-847` rejects host event dispatch unless the mode is DynamicInteractive. `findIntersecting`, `hitTestLink` and `cursorAt` (`donner/svg/DonnerController.cc:67-143`) do not check the mode. | `donner/svg/tests/SVGDocument_tests.cc:2941-2947` (secure animated rejects `dispatchAnimationEvent` and `beginAnimation`). | low | S | Animation triggering is gated. Link and cursor queries still answer for a secure document, so a host that forwards clicks would traverse links from an image-mode document unless it checks the mode itself. |
| feat:svg2/conform-processing-mode-set | partial | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/conform.html#processing-modes | Five processing modes: dynamic interactive, animated, secure animated, static and secure static, each fixing script, external references, animation and interaction. | `donner/svg/core/ProcessingMode.h:18-28` defines DynamicInteractive, SecureAnimated and SecureStatic. | See the row above. | See the row above. | low | S | Animated and static modes (external references allowed, no interaction) cannot be selected. Dynamic interactive mode in Donner still has script execution off. The reference-mode rules belong to `structure.md`. |

The engine has no currentScale or currentTranslate and applies no user zoom or pan to the document; the editor viewport zoom is application chrome outside the document. | None. | - | - | The CR removed the attribute from the attribute index and keeps one should-level sentence for the obsolete value. |

| feat:svg2/interact-text-selection | partial | https://www.w3.org/TR/2026/CR-SVG2-20261006/interact.html#event-processing | If the target is a text content element and the event is part of a selection gesture, the text selection algorithm runs. | n/a | `donner/svg/SVGTextContentElement.cc:140-142`: `selectSubString` is an empty stub. `getCharNumAtPosition` works (`donner/svg/text/TextEngine.h:248`). | `donner/svg/renderer/tests/TextEngineScripted_tests.cc:3457-3483` cover `getCharNumAtPosition` only. | low | L | No selection state, highlight painting, `::selection` styling or clipboard path in the engine. The editor text tool edits source text and is not SVG text selection. Classed partial, not not-applicable, because the positional primitive exists and the selection entry point is a declared stub. |

## Tests to create

- `val:pointer-events/bounding-box`, interact.html#PointerEventsProperty: a rect rotated 45 degrees with fill and stroke none; probe a corner of the document-space enclosing box that lies outside the rotated rect (must miss) and a point inside (must hit). Second case: wide stroke, probe just outside the fill box (must miss).
- `val:pointer-events/visibleStroke`, `visible`, `stroke`, `all`: rect with `stroke="none" stroke-width="20"`; probe on the outline band (must hit), and for visible and all also the interior.
- `val:pointer-events/fill`: `fill="none" visibility="hidden"` rect, interior probe must hit, stroke band beyond the fill must miss.
- `val:pointer-events/painted`: hidden but painted rect must hit.
- `feat:svg2/interact-hit-stroke-region`: line with `stroke-width="20"`; probe 9 units from the centerline (hit) and 15 units (miss); butt cap versus square cap past the endpoint; a dashed stroke gap; a percentage stroke width; `vector-effect="non-scaling-stroke"` under a scale.
- `feat:svg2/interact-hit-fill-region`: self-intersecting star with `fill-rule="evenodd"`, probe the center (miss) and a point (hit); same star with nonzero (center hits).
- `feat:svg2/interact-hit-image-rectangle` and `-transparent-pixels`: `image` with a data-URL PNG that has a transparent half; probe opaque and transparent pixels under auto (both hit), `visibility="hidden"` with auto (miss) and with `pointer-events="painted"` (hit), and `none` (miss); `a` wrapping the image resolves through `hitTestLink`.
- `feat:svg2/interact-hit-clip-path`: large rect clipped to a small circle over a second rect; probe outside the circle must return the lower rect.
- `feat:svg2/interact-hit-overflow-clip`: nested `svg` with a small viewport and overflowing content; record the chosen behavior for a probe outside the viewport.
- `feat:svg2/interact-hit-mask`, `-hit-filter`: rect with a mask that zeroes half of it, and rect with a filter that offsets its pixels; probes over the original geometry must hit.
- `feat:svg2/interact-hit-opacity`: `opacity="0"` and `stroke-opacity="0"` shapes must hit.
- `feat:svg2/interact-hit-text-character-cells`: two-line text with a wide gap, probe the gap inside the union ink box (must miss); probe a space character cell (must hit); `tspan` and inline `a` as targets.
- `feat:svg2/interact-hit-text-paint-and-visibility`: text with `fill="none" stroke="none"` must miss under auto and painted, hit under fill, stroke and all.
- `feat:svg2/interact-hit-root-svg-not-target`: root with `pointer-events="bounding-box"` and two separated rects; probe between them must return no element. Same for a nested `svg` (`-hit-nested-svg-not-target`).
- `feat:svg2/interact-hit-never-rendered`: probe over geometry that exists only inside `defs`, `clipPath` and `symbol`.
- `feat:svg2/interact-use-instance-listeners`: `rect id="r"` in defs with `animate begin="r.click"`, instantiated by `use`; dispatch click on the hit target of the instance and assert the animation starts.
- `feat:svg2/interact-a-nested-links-inert`: change the pinned expectation to the outer link once behavior is fixed.
- `feat:svg2/interact-a-link-in-text`: `text` containing `a href` around one word; `hitTestLink` over that word returns the link, over another word returns none.
- `feat:svg2/conform-interaction-disabled`: parse in SecureStatic and SecureAnimated; assert the intended result of `hitTestLink` and `dispatchMouseEvent`.
- `feat:svg2/interact-events-bubbling`: `begin="group.mouseenter"` must not fire for a mouseenter dispatched on a child; `begin="group.click"` must.
- `feat:svg2/interact-script-never-rendered`: top-level `script` with `style="display:block"` and text content renders no pixels.
- `feat:css-ui-4/cursor-ua-link-rule`: `cursorAt` over a shape inside `a href` returns pointer without author styling.
- `val:cursor/<url-set>`: `cursor: image-set("a.png" 1x, "b.png" 2x) 2 2, pointer` parses.
- `feat:svg2/interact-event-dynamic-pseudo-classes`: stylesheet `rect:hover { fill: red } rect { stroke: blue }` must keep the second rule, and `circle, rect:hover { fill: red }` must not be dropped silently; later, a hover state API drives matching.
- Attribute preservation, one parameterized test over a representative of each family (`role`, `aria-label`, `aria-hidden`, `onclick`, `onload`, `tabindex`, `autofocus`): value readable through `getAttribute`, matched by an attribute selector, present in `serializeToString` output, and removed with a warning under `disableUserAttributes`.
- `feat:svg2/access-title-accessible-name`, `-desc-accessible-description`, `-title-desc-language-selection`: title with nested markup flattens to plain text; several `title lang` siblings and a preferred-language input once an API exists.
- `feat:svg2/interact-focus-*`: once a focus-order query exists, `tabindex` ordering (positive before zero, document order ties), `a href` focusable by default, `display:none` excluded, `visibility:hidden` included, `use` instances ordered at the `use`.

## Unverified / could not fetch

- Nothing failed to fetch. WAI-ARIA, Core-AAM, Graphics-AAM, AccName, UI Events, DOM, HTML and Clipboard API were deliberately not fetched (see Specs read); statements about them are limited to what the SVG 2 CR and SVG-AAM text read here says.
- Whether the animation engine can target `role`, `aria-*` or `on*` attributes (the CR marks aria-* animatable, role and event attributes not) was not traced.
- Discrete animation of `pointer-events` and `cursor` was not traced.
- The claim that render instances are scanned in paint order rests on reading instance creation (`donner/svg/renderer/RenderingContext.cc:471-472`) and the reverse view iteration (`:1836-1841`), not on a run.
- `feat:svg2/interact-use-instance-listeners` is a code-reading conclusion with no test either way.
- Whether the editor preview forwards `mousemove` and `wheel` to `dispatchMouseEvent` was not traced.
- Flattening of markup nested inside `title` and `desc` was not traced beyond the comment at `donner/svg/parser/SVGParser.cc:395-403`.
- How an unknown CSS property such as `touch-action` is reported (silently ignored or with a warning) was not re-verified here.
